Thus algorithm:
Initialize a,b arrays zeros length 2n-1.
For each d in -n+1..n-1:
 idx=n-1+d
 For each x in [i,j], delta = α sign (+1 for i, -1 for j):
   oldx = h[x]
   if d>=0:
     if x >= d:  # x as first, other y=x-d as second
        other = h[x-d]
        a[idx] += delta * (1 - other)
     if d==0: # x as second would double; instead handle once.
     else if x <= n-1-d: # x as second, other=x+d first
        other = h[x+d]
        a[idx] -= delta * other  # because coeff -δ*h_other
   else:
     e=-d
     if x <= n-1-e: # x as first, other=x+e second
        a[idx] += delta * (1 - h[x+e])
     if x >= e: # x as second, other=x-e first
        a[idx] -= delta * h[x-e]

```
def project_anti(h):
```

Function `pair_feasible_intervals(Y)` returns list of (lo,hi) maybe merged, or None.
Implementation:
```
def _prune_interval(lo,hi): return if hi >= lo - tol.
def pair_feasible_intervals(Y):
    if P_ZERO_MAX > Y + 1e-12: return []
    intervals=[]
    lo0=P_LO; hi0=P_HI
    if hi0 < lo0 - 1e-12: return []
    # linear pos
    if P_POS_INV.size:
        b=Y*P_POS_INV - P_POS_C0INV
        hi_lim=float(np.min(b))
        hi0=min(hi0, hi_lim)
    if hi0 < lo0 - tol: return []
    if P_NEG_INV.size:
        b=Y*P_NEG_INV - P_NEG_C0INV
        lo_lim=float(np.max(b))
        lo0=max(lo0, lo_lim)
    if hi0 < lo0 - tol: return []
    intervals=[(lo0,hi0)]
    # quadratics
    for each q:
       new=[]
       a=P_QUAD_B[k]; bcoef=P_QUAD_A[k]; c=P_CORR[P_QUAD_IDX[k]] - Y
       if abs(a) < tol: # linear but should not
          if bcoef>0: hi0? ... handle
       else:
          disc = bcoef*bcoef - 4*a*c
       if disc < -1e-10: 
           if a > 0: return [] # no sol
           # if a<0 all real; continue
           continue
       if disc < 0: disc=0
       sqrt=np.sqrt(disc)
       r1=(-bcoef - sqrt)/(2*a); r2=(-bcoef + sqrt)/(2*a)
       if r1 > r2: swap
       if a > 0:
          for il,ih in intervals:
             nl=max(il,r1); nh=min(ih,r2); if nh>=nl-tol add
       else:
          for il,ih:
             # left
             if il <= r2? Actually outside: il<=r1 or ih>=r2.
             # if ih <= r1: [max(il?), min(ih,r1)]
             if ih > r1 - tol: # may intersect left
                nl=max(il, -1e300); nh=min(ih, r1); if nh>=nl add
             if il < r2 + tol:
                nl=max(il, r2); nh=ih; if nh>=nl add
       if not new: return []
       # merge new
       intervals=merge
    return intervals
```
For a>0 if disc==0 point root; intersection okay.
For a<0 and disc==0, all real (max touches 0); continue (no restriction). In code if disc <= tol and a<0: continue.
Maybe for a>0 disc very small: root point, intervals tiny.

Precompute for pair:
```
def build_pair_coeff(i,j,h,corr):
 n=len(h); a=np.zeros(2*n-1); b=np.zeros(2*n-1)
 for d in range(-(n-1), n):
   idx=n-1+d
   q_start = 0 if d<0? Wait unified: q_start = max(0, -d)
   q_end = n-1 - max(0, d)
   # for x=i delta=1
   vals=[(i,1.0),(j,-1.0)]
   for x,delta in vals:
      # as p: q=x-d
      q = x - d
      if q_start <= q <= q_end:
          other = h[q]
          a[idx] += delta * (1.0 - other)
      # as q:
      if q_start <= x <= q_end:
          other = h[x + d]
          a[idx] -= delta * other
      if d == 0:
          b[idx] -= delta*delta
   if d !=0 and abs(d) == abs(i-j):
       b[idx] += 1.0
 return a,b
```
Check d=0 for both i and j: if i and j different, d=0 loop x=i adds a=1-2hi,b=-1; x=j adds, b=-2. Good. Cross d=abs diff: only one idx? d = i-j or j-i exactly. We add +1 for d=abs if `abs(d)==abs(i-j)` but there are two d values ±diff. Should cross exist for both orientations? C_d for d= i-j has term p=i,q=j. C_{-diff} has term p? For d=-diff, p-q=d, if i,j diff, p=j,q=i? yes term h_j(1-h_i). Both affected with cross? Wait c_{-diff} includes pair p=j,q=i; both changed. So b_cross at d=+diff and d=-diff both +1. The condition `abs(d)==abs(i-j)` adds +1 for both signs. Correct.

Pass structure:
```
def pair_descent(h,n,deadline,seed):
 h=project_feasible(h,n)
 corr=np.correlate(h,1-h)
 current_max_corr=np.max(corr)
 best_local_corr=current_max_corr
 improved_pass=True
 offsets = [1,2,3, n//4?] etc maybe random.
 while time.time() < deadline - 5 and improved_pass:
    improved_pass=False
    # deterministic offsets for maybe first cycles
    offsets = [1, max(1,n//8), max(1,n//4), max(1,n//2)]
    for off in offsets:
      for i in range(n-1): j=(i+off) % n; if i<j: do_pair
    # random pairs
    num_random = n
    for _ in range(num_random): i,j=random distinct; do_pair
    if current_max_corr < best_local_corr - 1e-12: best_local_corr = current_max_corr; if time check evaluate exact? maybe.
 return h, current_max_corr*dx
```
But after each pair update, current_max_corr changes. We can update best_local. For `do_pair`, needs current corr and h. If no pairs improve in that pass, break (local min for sampled pairs). Let's design `project_feasible` carefully.
Input h length n maybe even. Need output sum exactly n/2 (float exact? Floating exact impossible but bitwise? Use final correction to one coord to make `np.sum(out) == target`? Sum of floats may not exactly equal target due representation, but we can adjust so computed sum is target by setting one coordinate to target - sum(others). That may be within [0,1]. Use loop.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.array(h,float).copy()
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 target=n/2.0
 # iterative adjust free coords
 for _ in range(100):
   diff=target - h.sum()
   if abs(diff) <= 1e-14: break
   mask=(h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14)
   if mask.any(): h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
   elif n: h += diff/n
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # exact final: adjust coordinate closest to 0.5 multiple times
 for _ in range(10):
   diff=target - h.sum()
   if abs(diff) <= 1e-15: break
   idx=int(np.argmin(np.abs(h-0.5)))
   val=h[idx]+diff
   if 0<=val<=1: h[idx]=val
   else:
     # distribute to free
     for j in range(n): ...
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 return h
```
But if h sum not exact due float, final adjust may break due float rounding. We can set one element to `target - np.sum(h without idx)`. But `np.sum` order. For i chosen:
```
s = h.sum() - h[i]
h[i] = target - s
```
Then `h.sum()` might not equal target due summation order, but close. The task likely uses `sum(h)*dx ≈1` with tolerance. Previous used this. OK.

```
def project_anti(h):
 n=len(h); out=np.empty(n)
 m=n//2

   z=0.5*(h[i] + (1.0 - h[n-1-i]))

   out[i]=z; out[n-1-i]=1.0-z
 if n%2: out[m]=0.5
 return project_feasible(out,n)
```

Let's implement `pair_descent` with time and sampling.
```
def pair_descent(h,n,deadline,seed):
 global _... maybe not.
 h=project_feasible(h,n)
 corr=compute_corr(h)
 best_corr=float(np.max(corr))
 local_corr=best_corr
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 pass_count=0
 improved_any=True
 while time.time() < deadline - 2 and improved_any:
   improved_any=False
   # deterministic offsets list depends on n
   offsets=[1,2,3, max(1,n//16), max(1,n//8), max(1,n//4), max(1,n//2)]
   # plus random offsets each pass
   rand_offsets=[int(rng.randint(1,n-1)) for _ in range(3)]
   for off in offsets+rand_offsets:
     if time.time() > deadline -2: break
     # if off=0 skip
     for i in range(n):
       j=(i+off)%n
       if i<j:
         if time.time() > deadline -1: break
         h2,corr2,imp = optimize_pair(h,corr,i,j)
         if imp:
           h=project_feasible? no. if sum drift maybe. h=h2; corr=corr2
           imp_corr=float(np.max(corr))
           if imp_corr < best_corr: best_corr=imp_corr
           if imp_corr < local_corr: local_corr=imp_corr
           improved_any=True
```

```
def compute_corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
```

```
def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; return float(np.max(compute_corr(h))*dx)
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if n==0: return h
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(60):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
        mask=(h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0-1e-12)
        cnt=float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt>0:
            h[mask] += diff/cnt
        else:
            h += diff/n
        h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # final exact adjust loop
    for _ in range(20):
        s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-15: break
        # choose index with room in direction
        if diff > 0:
            room=1.0-h
            idx=int(np.argmax(room))
        else:
            room=h
            idx=int(np.argmax(room))
        newv=h[idx]-diff? Wait need h[idx] + diff. If diff negative, reduce. Check bounds:
        newv = h[idx] + diff
        if 0 <= newv <=1:
            # set exactly to target - sum of others
            s_other = h.sum() - h[idx]
            h[idx] = target - s_other
            # may slightly out due float, clip
        else:
            # distribute to multiple with room
            if diff>0:
                order=np.argsort(-h) # more room? room 1-h
            else:
                order=np.argsort(h) # less value more room down
            added=0.0
            for kk in order:
                rem=target - (h.sum() - h[kk]?) This is messy.
```

`random_smooth_candidate(n,seed)`:

```
def random_smooth_candidate(n,seed):
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
 s=np.zeros(n)
 maxfreq=8
 for k in range(1,maxfreq+1):
    amp = (1.0/(k+1.0))*rng.randn()
    s += amp*np.cos(np.pi*k*x + 2*np.pi*rng.rand())
    s += amp*np.sin(np.pi*k*x + 2*np.pi*rng.rand())
 s = 0.35*np.tanh(2.0*s)
 h=0.5+s
 return project_feasible(h,n)
```

`build_pair_coeff(h, i, j)` returns a,b. Need use current h. For speed, avoid allocating full arrays? We need full for feasibility. Use zeros length m=2*n-1.
Loop:
```
def build_pair_coeff(h,i,j):
 n=len(h); m=2*n-1; a=np.zeros(m); b=np.zeros(m)
 if i==j: return a,b
 di=abs(i-j)
 for d in range(-(n-1), n):
    idx=n-1+d
    q_start = 0
    if d < 0: q_start = -d
    max0d = d if d>0 else 0
    q_end = n - 1 - (d if d>0 else 0)
    # x=i, delta=1
    x=i; delta=1.0
    q = x - d
    if q_start <= q <= q_end:
        a[idx] += delta * (1.0 - h[q])
    if q_start <= x <= q_end:
        a[idx] -= delta * h[x + d]
    if d == 0:
        b[idx] -= delta*delta
    # x=j delta=-1
    x=j; delta=-1.0
    q = x - d
    if q_start <= q <= q_end:
        a[idx] += delta * (1.0 - h[q])
    if q_start <= x <= q_end:
        a[idx] -= delta * h[x + d]
    if d == 0:
        b[idx] -= delta*delta
    if d != 0 and abs(d) == di:
        b[idx] += 1.0
 return a,b
```
Check x+d index: For as q condition, q=x in range, p=x+d is valid by q range; no out of bounds. Good.
For d=0, condition as p q=x true, as q true, a add (1-h)-h=1-2h; b -1. For j similarly.
For d nonzero, if coordinate appears as p and q in different terms, both conditions may true; a sum correct. If pair cross at d=±di, b add 1. But if d=±di and also coordinate appears in other terms, b independent.
Potential double count b_cross if i and j? For d=di, cross term exists; for d=-di, cross term exists. Good.

`setup_pair_globals(corr,a,b,lo,hi)`:

```
def setup_pair_globals(corr,a,b,lo,hi):
 global _P_CORR,...
 _P_CORR=corr; _P_A=a; _P_B=b; ...
 tol=1e-13
 lin = np.abs(b) <= tol
 pos = lin & (a > tol)
 neg = lin & (a < -tol)
 zero = lin & (np.abs(a) <= tol)
 if zero.any(): _P_ZERO_MAX=float(np.max(corr[zero]))
 else: _P_ZERO_MAX=-1e300
 if pos.any():
   ap=a[pos]; c0=corr[pos]
   _P_POS_INV=1.0/ap; _P_POS_C0INV=c0/ap
 else: empty arrays
 ...
 qmask = np.abs(b) > tol
 idxs=np.nonzero(qmask)[0]
 _P_QUAD_IDX=idxs; _P_QUAD_A=a[idxs]; _P_QUAD_B=b[idxs]; _P_QUAD_C=corr[idxs]
 _P_LO=lo; _P_HI=hi
```

`prune_intervals(intervals)`:

```
def merge_intervals(intervals, tol=1e-12):
 if not intervals: return []
 intervals.sort()
 merged=[]
 curlo,curhi=intervals[0]
 for lo,hi in intervals[1:]:
   if lo <= curhi + tol:
      if hi > curhi: curhi=hi
   else:
      merged.append((curlo,curhi)); curlo,curhi=lo,hi
 merged.append((curlo,curhi))
 return [(lo,hi) for lo,hi in merged if hi >= lo - tol]
```

`pair_feasible_intervals(Y)`:
Need use globals. Let's write robust:
```
def pair_feasible_intervals(Y):
    tol=1e-12
    if _P_ZERO_MAX > Y + tol:
        return []
    lo=_P_LO; hi=_P_HI
    if hi < lo - tol: return []
    if _P_POS_INV.size:
        bounds = Y * _P_POS_INV - _P_POS_C0INV
        hi_lim = float(np.min(bounds))
        if hi_lim < hi: hi=hi_lim
    if hi < lo - tol: return []
    if _P_NEG_INV.size:
        bounds = Y * _P_NEG_INV - _P_NEG_C0INV
        lo_lim = float(np.max(bounds))
        if lo_lim > lo: lo=lo_lim
    if hi < lo - tol: return []
    intervals=[(lo,hi)]
    if _P_QUAD_IDX.size:
       for k in range(_P_QUAD_IDX.size):
          idx=_P_QUAD_IDX[k]; a=_P_QUAD_B[k]; bcoef=_P_QUAD_A[k]; c=_P_QUAD_C[k]-Y
          new=[]
          if abs(a) < tol:
             lin=bcoef*alpha + c <=0? But b=0 excluded; handle linear to be safe.
             if abs(bcoef) < tol:
                if c <= tol: continue # no restriction
                else return []
             if bcoef > 0:
                # alpha <= -c/bcoef
                for il,ih in intervals:
                   nl=il; nh=min(ih, -c/bcoef); if nh>=nl-tol new
             else:
                for ... nl=max(il, -c/bcoef)
          else:
             disc=bcoef*bcoef - 4.0*a*c
             if disc < -1e-10:
                if a > 0: return []
                # a<0 all feasible
                continue
             if disc < 0: disc=0.0
             sqrt=np.sqrt(disc)
             r1=(-bcoef - sqrt)/(2.0*a)
             r2=(-bcoef + sqrt)/(2.0*a)
             if r1 > r2: r1,r2=r2,r1
             if a > 0:
                for il,ih in intervals:
                   nl=max(il,r1); nh=min(ih,r2)
                   if nh >= nl - tol: new.append((nl,nh))
             else:
                for il,ih in intervals:
                   # left part il <= r1
                   if ih >= r1 - tol:
                      nl=il; nh=min(ih, r1)
                      if nh >= nl - tol: new.append((nl,nh))
                   # right part ih >= r2
                   if il <= r2 + tol:
                      nl=max(il, r2); nh=ih
                      if nh >= nl - tol: new.append((nl,nh))
          if not new: return []
          intervals=merge_intervals(new, tol)
    return intervals
```

`optimize_pair(h,corr,i,j)`:
```
def optimize_pair(h, corr, i, j):
    n=len(h)
    if i==j: return h,corr,False
    hi=float(h[i]); hj=float(h[j])
    amin=max(-hi, hj-1.0)
    amax=min(1.0-hi, hj)
    if amax <= amin + 1e-12:
        return h,corr,False
    a,b=build_pair_coeff(h,i,j)
    setup_pair_globals(corr,a,b,amin,amax)
    current_max = float(np.max(corr))
    base = _P_ZERO_MAX
    if base < -1e200: base=0.0
    if base >= current_max - 1e-12:
        return h,corr,False
    # if base > amax? no Y not alpha
    # Test base feasibility
    if pair_feasible_intervals(base):
        hiY=base
        alpha=pair_choose_alpha(base)
    else:
        loY=base
        hiY=current_max + 1e-9
        # if hi not feasible due numeric, increase
        if not pair_feasible_intervals(hiY):
            hiY=current_max + 1.0
            if not pair_feasible_intervals(hiY):
                return h,corr,False
        # maybe loY feasible? We already tested base false. But if base <0? no.
        span=hiY-loY
        for _ in range(45):
            mid=(loY+hiY)*0.5
            if hiY - loY < 1e-10 * max(1.0, hiY): break
            if pair_feasible_intervals(mid):
                hiY=mid
            else:
                loY=mid
        alpha=pair_choose_alpha(hiY)
    # tighten alpha
    alpha = float(alpha)
    alpha = min(max(alpha, amin + 1e-13), amax - 1e-13)
    if not (amin <= alpha <= amax): alpha=0.0
    if abs(alpha) < 1e-14:
        return h,corr,False
    new_corr = corr + a*alpha + b*alpha*alpha
    new_max = float(np.max(new_corr))
    if new_max < current_max - 1e-10:
        h_new = h.copy()
        h_new[i] += alpha
        h_new[j] -= alpha
        # tiny bound fix
        if h_new[i] < 0.0:
            if h_new[i] > -1e-12: h_new[i]=0.0
            else: return h,corr,False
        elif h_new[i] > 1.0:
            if h_new[i] < 1.0+1e-12: h_new[i]=1.0
            else: return h,corr,False
        if h_new[j] < 0.0: ...
        # If clipped, recompute corr exact? clipping tiny; to be safe if any clip magnitude >1e-13 recompute.
        if ...:
           h_new=np.clip(h_new,0,1)
           # sum exact? pair sum might off by tiny; project? Could call project and recompute.
           # Since only tiny, recompute corr.
           h_new=project_feasible(h_new,n)
           new_corr=compute_corr(h_new)
           new_max=float(np.max(new_corr))
           if new_max >= current_max - 1e-10: return h,corr,False
        # optionally if sum error recompute/project
        sum_err = h_new.sum() - n/2.0
        if abs(sum_err) > 1e-10:
           h_new=project_feasible(h_new,n)
           new_corr=compute_corr(h_new)
           new_max=float(np.max(new_corr))
        corr = new_corr
        h = h_new
        return h, corr, True
    return h,corr,False
```
But note `new_corr` computed using old corr; if we project, recompute. If no project, sum drift tiny. Good.

`pair_descent` details:
```
def pair_descent(h,n,deadline,seed):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    corr=compute_corr(h)
    best_corr=float(np.max(corr))
    local_corr=best_corr
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_improve=0
    pass_num=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        improved_pass=False
        # deterministic offsets
        offs=set([1,2,3,5,8,13,21,34,55])
        offs.add(max(1,n//2))
        offs.add(max(1,n//4))
        offs.add(max(1,n//8))
        # random offsets
        for _ in range(4): offs.add(int(rng.randint(1,n)))
        for off in list(offs):
           off=min(off,n-off)
           if off==0: continue
           for i in range(n):
               j=(i+off)%n
               if i<j:
                   if time.time() > deadline - 1.0: break
                   h,corr,imp=optimize_pair(h,corr,i,j)
                   if imp:
                       improved_pass=True
                       cm=float(np.max(corr))
                       if cm < best_corr: best_corr=cm
                       if cm < local_corr: local_corr=cm
               if time.time() > deadline - 1.0: break
           if time.time() > deadline -1.0: break
        # random pairs
        num_rand = max(20, n)
        for _ in range(num_rand):
           if time.time() > deadline-1.0: break
           i=int(rng.randint(0,n)); j=int(rng.randint(0,n))
           if i==j: continue
           if i>j: i,j=j,i
           h,corr,imp=optimize_pair(h,corr,i,j)
           if imp: ...
        pass_num+=1
        # if no improvement, maybe change noise? If repeated no improve, break or do random perturbation.
        if not improved_pass:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve >= 2:
               break
        else:
           no_improve=0
        # clean corr periodically
        if pass_num % 5 == 0 and time.time() < deadline - 2:
           corr=compute_corr(h)
           local_corr=float(np.max(corr))
           if local_corr < best_corr: best_corr=local_corr
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    return h, compute_c5(h)
```

```
def smooth_max_obj(x):
    n=_N_POLISH; beta=_BETA_POLISH; dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full')
    m=np.max(corr)
    shifted=np.minimum(beta*(corr-m),0.0)
    return (m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shifted)))/beta)*dx
```

```
def optimize_candidate(h,n,deadline,seed):
    if time.time() >= deadline: return h, compute_c5(h)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5(h)
    if deadline - time.time() > 30:
        h2,c2=pair_descent(h,n,deadline-5,seed)
        if c2 < c: h,c=h2,c2
    # maybe final small pair if time? no
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5(h)
    return h,c
```

Now, code for `project_feasible` final exact loop: Let's implement robust with while:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,float).copy()
    if n==0: return h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(50):
        diff=target - np.sum(h)
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14: break
        mask=(h > 1e-15) & (h < 1.0-1e-15)
        cnt=np.count_nonzero(mask)
        if cnt>0:
            h[mask] += diff/cnt
        elif n>0:
            h += diff/n
        h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # distribute residue exactly
    diff=target - np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff) > 1e-16:
       if diff > 0:
          order=np.argsort(1.0 - h) # least remaining? We want large room: argsort of -(1-h)= h? Actually to allocate diff small, choose max room: order=np.argsort(h) gives small h first (max 1-h). Wait if h small, room large. So diff>0: order=np.argsort(h) (low h first). diff<0: order=np.argsort(-h) (high h first) or argsort(-h) decreasing? np.argsort(-h) gives high h first.
       else:
          order=np.argsort(-h)
       for idx in order:
           if diff == 0: break
           if diff > 0:
              add=min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
              if add > 0: h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
           else:
              add=min(-diff, h[idx])
              if add>0: h[idx]-=add; diff+=add
    # final one adjustment for float
    diff=target - np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff)>1e-16:
       if diff>0: idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
       else: idx=int(np.argmax(h))
       newv=h[idx]+diff
       if 0<=newv<=1: h[idx]=newv
    return h
```
If diff not fully allocated due no room (shouldn't after iterative), final one may out of bounds; ignore. Sum close.

```
def random_smooth_candidate(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    s=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,9):
        amp=0.6/(k+1.0)
        ang1=2.0*np.pi*rng.rand()
        ang2=2.0*np.pi*rng.rand()
        s += amp*np.cos(np.pi*k*x + ang1) * rng.randn()
        s += amp*np.sin(np.pi*k*x + ang2) * rng.randn()
    h=0.5 + 0.5*np.tanh(1.8*s)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

Now, detailed pair_descent with time checks:
```
def pair_descent(h, n, deadline, seed):
    if n <= 0: return h, float('inf')
    if deadline > _RUN_DEADLINE: deadline = _RUN_DEADLINE
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    current_max = float(np.max(corr))
    active = np.where(corr >= current_max - 1e-8)[0]
    best_local = current_max
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_improve = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 0.5:
       improved = False
       # targeted pairs
       num_target = max(50, n)
       for _ in range(num_target):
           if time.time() > deadline - 0.5: break
           if active.size == 0: break
           idx = active[int(rng.randint(active.size))]
           d = int(idx - (n - 1))
           qs = 0 if d < 0 else 0? Need q_start = max(0,-d); q_end = n-1 - max(0,d)
           if q_end < qs: continue
           q = qs + int(rng.randint(0, q_end - qs + 1))
           p = q + d
           if p == q or p < 0 or p >= n or q < 0 or q >= n: continue
           # p,q may be unordered; optimize_pair handles any
           h,corr,imp = optimize_pair(h,corr,p,q,active)
           if imp:
              improved=True
              cm=float(np.max(corr))
              if cm < best_local: best_local=cm
              active=np.where(corr >= cm - 1e-8)[0]
           if time.time() > deadline - 0.5: break
       # deterministic offsets maybe smaller number
       if time.time() < deadline - 2:
          offs = {1,2,3, max(1,n//8), max(1,n//4), max(1,n//2)}
          for off in list(offs):
             off = min(off, n-off)
             if off == 0: continue
             for i in range(n):
                if time.time() > deadline - 1: break
                j=(i+off)%n
                if i<j:
                   h,corr,imp=optimize_pair(h,corr,i,j,active)
                   if imp: ...
       # random pairs
       num_rand = max(20, n//2)
       for _ in range(num_rand): ...
       if not improved:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve >= 2: break
       else:
          no_improve=0
       # clean
       if time.time() < deadline - 1:
          corr=compute_corr(h)
          cm=float(np.max(corr))
          active=np.where(corr >= cm - 1e-8)[0]
          if cm < best_local: best_local=cm
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5(h)
    return h,c
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_RUN_DEADLINE = 0.0

_P_CORR = None
_P_LO = 0.0
_P_HI = 0.0
_P_POS_INV = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
_P_POS_C0INV = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
_P_NEG_INV = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
_P_NEG_C0INV = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
_P_ZERO_MAX = -1e300
_P_QUAD_IDX = np.empty(0, dtype=int)
_P_QUAD_A = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
_P_QUAD_B = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
_P_QUAD_C = np.empty(0, dtype=float)


def time_left():
    return max(0.0, _RUN_DEADLINE - time.time())


def compute_corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return float('inf')
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = compute_corr(h)
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if n == 0:
        return h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(n) / 2.0

    for _ in range(60):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
            break
        free = (h > 1e-15) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-15)
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[free] += diff / float(cnt)
        else:
            h += diff / float(n)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-16:
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(h)
        else:
            order = np.argsort(-h)
        for idx in order:
            if abs(diff) <= 1e-16:
                break
            if diff > 0.0:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
                if add > 0.0:
                    h[idx] += add
                    diff -= add
            else:
                add = min(-diff, h[idx])
                if add > 0.0:
                    h[idx] -= add
                    diff += add

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-16:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
        nv = h[idx] + diff
        if 0.0 <= nv <= 1.0:
            h[idx] = nv
    return h


def project_anti(h):
    n = len(h)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    m = n // 2
    for i in range(m):
        z = 0.5 * (h[i] + (1.0 - h[n - 1 - i]))
        z = float(np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0))
        out[i] = z
        out[n - 1 - i] = 1.0 - z
    if n % 2 == 1:
        out[m] = 0.5
    return project_feasible(out, n)


def project_sym(h):
    n = len(h)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    m = n // 2
    for i in range(m):
        v = 0.5 * (h[i] + h[n - 1 - i])
        out[i] = v
        out[n - 1 - i] = v
    if n % 2 == 1:
        out[m] = h[m]
    return project_feasible(out, n)


def interp_to(src, src_n, dst_n):
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=float)
    if src_n == dst_n:
        return src.copy()
    xp = (np.arange(src_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(src_n))
    xn = (np.arange(dst_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(dst_n))
    return np.interp(xn, xp, src)


def basic_candidate(n, seed):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def random_smooth_candidate(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for k in range(1, 9):
        amp = 0.7 / float(k + 1)
        a = amp * rng.randn()
        b = amp * rng.randn()
        ang1 = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.rand()
        ang2 = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.rand()
        s += a * np.cos(np.pi * k * x + ang1)
        s += b * np.sin(np.pi * k * x + ang2)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(2.4 * s)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def random_binary_candidate(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = (rng.rand(n) < 0.5).astype(float)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def add_candidate(cands, h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5(h)
    cands.append((float(c), h.copy(), int(n)))
    return c


def merge_intervals(intervals, tol=1e-12):
    if not intervals:
        return []
    intervals = [tuple(x) for x in intervals]
    intervals.sort()
    merged = []
    clo, chi = intervals[0]
    for lo, hi in intervals[1:]:
        if lo <= chi + tol:
            if hi > chi:
                chi = hi
        else:
            merged.append((clo, chi))
            clo, chi = lo, hi
    merged.append((clo, chi))
    out = []
    for lo, hi in merged:
        if hi >= lo - tol:
            out.append((float(lo), float(hi)))
    return out


def setup_pair_globals(corr, a, b, lo, hi):
    global _P_CORR, _P_LO, _P_HI
    global _P_POS_INV, _P_POS_C0INV
    global _P_NEG_INV, _P_NEG_C0INV
    global _P_ZERO_MAX
    global _P_QUAD_IDX, _P_QUAD_A, _P_QUAD_B, _P_QUAD_C

    _P_CORR = corr
    _P_LO = float(lo)
    _P_HI = float(hi)

    tol = 1e-13
    lin = np.abs(b) <= tol
    pos = lin & (a > tol)
    neg = lin & (a < -tol)
    zero = lin & (np.abs(a) <= tol)

    if zero.any():
        _P_ZERO_MAX = float(np.max(corr[zero]))
    else:
        _P_ZERO_MAX = -1e300

    if pos.any():
        ap = a[pos]
        cp = corr[pos]
        _P_POS_INV = 1.0 / ap
        _P_POS_C0INV = cp / ap
    else:
        _P_POS_INV = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
        _P_POS_C0INV = np.empty(0, dtype=float)

    if neg.any():
        an = a[neg]
        cn = corr[neg]
        _P_NEG_INV = 1.0 / an
        _P_NEG_C0INV = cn / an
    else:
        _P_NEG_INV = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
        _P_NEG_C0INV = np.empty(0, dtype=float)

    qmask = np.abs(b) > tol
    qidx = np.nonzero(qmask)[0]
    _P_QUAD_IDX = qidx
    _P_QUAD_A = a[qidx]
    _P_QUAD_B = b[qidx]
    _P_QUAD_C = corr[qidx]


def pair_feasible_intervals(Y):
    tol = 1e-12
    if _P_ZERO_MAX > Y + tol:
        return []

    lo = _P_LO
    hi = _P_HI
    if hi < lo - tol:
        return []

    if _P_POS_INV.size:
        bounds = Y * _P_POS_INV - _P_POS_C0INV
        hi_lim = float(np.min(bounds))
        if hi_lim < hi:
            hi = hi_lim
    if hi < lo - tol:
        return []

    if _P_NEG_INV.size:
        bounds = Y * _P_NEG_INV - _P_NEG_C0INV
        lo_lim = float(np.max(bounds))
        if lo_lim > lo:
            lo = lo_lim
    if hi < lo - tol:
        return []

    intervals = [(lo, hi)]

    if _P_QUAD_IDX.size:
        for k in range(_P_QUAD_IDX.size):
            qcoef = float(_P_QUAD_B[k])
            lincoef = float(_P_QUAD_A[k])
            c0 = float(_P_QUAD_C[k]) - Y
            new_ints = []

            if abs(qcoef) < tol:
                if abs(lincoef) < tol:
                    if c0 > tol:
                        return []
                elif lincoef > 0.0:
                    bound = -c0 / lincoef
                    for il, ih in intervals:
                        nh = min(ih, bound)
                        if nh >= il - tol:
                            new_ints.append((il, nh))
                else:
                    bound = -c0 / lincoef
                    for il, ih in intervals:
                        nl = max(il, bound)
                        if ih >= nl - tol:
                            new_ints.append((nl, ih))
                if not new_ints:
                    return []
                intervals = merge_intervals(new_int, tol)
                continue

            disc = lincoef * lincoef - 4.0 * qcoef * c0
            if disc < -1e-10:
                if qcoef > 0.0:
                    return []
                # concave with no real roots is always <= 0
                continue
            if disc < 0.0:
                disc = 0.0

            sq = float(np.sqrt(disc))
            r1 = (-lincoef - sq) / (2.0 * qcoef)
            r2 = (-lincoef + sq) / (2.0 * qcoef)
            if r1 > r2:
                r1, r2 = r2, r1

            if qcoef > 0.0:
                for il, ih in intervals:
                    nl = max(il, r1)
                    nh = min(ih, r2)
                    if nh >= nl - tol:
                        new_ints.append((nl, nh))
            else:
                for il, ih in intervals:
                    if ih >= r1 - tol:
                        nl = il
                        nh = min(ih, r1)
                        if nh >= nl - tol:
                            new_ints.append((nl, nh))
                    if il <= r2 + tol:
                        nl = max(il, r2)
                        nh = ih
                        if nh >= nl - tol:
                            new_ints.append((nl, nh))

            if not new_ints:
                return []
            intervals = merge_intervals(new_int, tol)

    return intervals


def pair_choose_alpha(Y):
    ints = pair_feasible_intervals(Y)
    if not ints:
        return 0.0
    for il, ih in ints:
        if il - 1e-12 <= 0.0 <= ih + 1e-12:
            return 0.0
    lo, hi = ints[0]
    if hi < lo:
        lo, hi = hi, lo
    val = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    if val < _P_LO:
        val = _P_LO
    if val > _P_HI:
        val = _P_HI
    return float(val)


def build_pair_coeff(h, i, j):
    n = len(h)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    a = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    b = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    if i == j:
        return a, b

    di = abs(i - j)
    for d in range(-(n - 1), n):
        idx = n - 1 + d
        if d < 0:
            q_start = -d
        else:
            q_start = 0
        if d > 0:
            q_end = n - 1 - d
        else:
            q_end = n - 1

        # coordinate i changes by +alpha
        x = i
        delta = 1.0
        q = x - d
        if q_start <= q <= q_end:
            a[idx] += delta * (1.0 - h[q])
        if q_start <= x <= q_end:
            a[idx] -= delta * h[x + d]
        if d == 0:
            b[idx] -= delta * delta

        # coordinate j changes by -alpha
        x = j
        delta = -1.0
        q = x - d
        if q_start <= q <= q_end:
            a[idx] += delta * (1.0 - h[q])
        if q_start <= x <= q_end:
            a[idx] -= delta * h[x + d]
        if d == 0:
            b[idx] -= delta * delta

        if d != 0 and abs(d) == di:
            b[idx] += 1.0

    return a, b


def optimize_pair(h, corr, i, j, active):
    n = len(h)
    if i == j:
        return h, corr, False
    if i < 0 or i >= n or j < 0 or j >= n:
        return h, corr, False

    hi = float(h[i])
    hj = float(h[j])
    amin = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
    amax = min(1.0 - hi, hj)
    if amax <= amin + 1e-12:
        return h, corr, False

    current_max = float(np.max(corr))
    if active is None or active.size == 0:
        active = np.where(corr >= current_max - 1e-8)[0]

    m1 = max(i, n - 1 - i)
    m2 = max(j, n - 1 - j)
    s = min(n - 1 - m1, n - 1 - m2)
    e = max(n - 1 + m1, n - 1 + m2)
    if active.size and not bool(np.any((active >= s) & (active <= e))):
        return h, corr, False

    a, b = build_pair_coeff(h, i, j)
    setup_pair_globals(corr, a, b, amin, amax)

    base = _P_ZERO_MAX
    if not np.isfinite(base) or base < 0.0:
        base = 0.0
    if base >= current_max - 1e-11:
        return h, corr, False

    if pair_feasible_intervals(base):
        hiY = base
        alpha = pair_choose_alpha(base)
    else:
        loY = base
        hiY = current_max + 1e-9
        if not pair_feasible_intervals(hiY):
            hiY = current_max + 1.0
            if not pair_feasible_intervals(hiY):
                return h, corr, False

        for _ in range(45):
            if hiY - loY <= 1e-10 * max(1.0, hiY):
                break
            mid = 0.5 * (loY + hiY)
            if pair_feasible_intervals(mid):
                hiY = mid
            else:
                loY = mid
        alpha = pair_choose_alpha(hiY)

    alpha = float(alpha)
    if not (amin <= alpha <= amax):
        alpha = min(max(alpha, amin), amax)
    alpha = min(max(alpha, amin + 1e-13), amax - 1e-13)
    if abs(alpha) < 1e-14:
        return h, corr, False

    new_corr = corr + a * alpha + b * alpha * alpha
    new_max = float(np.max(new_corr))
    if new_max >= current_max - 1e-10:
        return h, corr, False

    h_new = h.copy()
    h_new[i] += alpha
    h_new[j] -= alpha

    bad = False
    if h_new[i] < 0.0 or h_new[j] < 0.0 or h_new[i] > 1.0 or h_new[j] > 1.0:
        if (abs(h_new[i]) <= 1e-10 and abs(h_new[j]) <= 1e-10 and
                abs(h_new[i] - 1.0) <= 1e-10 and abs(h_new[j] - 1.0) <= 1e-10):
            h_new = np.clip(h_new, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            bad = True
    if bad:
        return h, corr, False

    if abs(float(np.sum(h_new)) - n / 2.0) > 1e-9:
        h_new = project_feasible(h_new, n)
        new_corr = compute_corr(h_new)
        new_max = float(np.max(new_corr))
        if new_max >= current_max - 1e-10:
            return h, corr, False

    return h_new, new_corr, True


def pair_descent(h, n, deadline, seed):
    if deadline > _RUN_DEADLINE:
        deadline = _RUN_DEADLINE
    if n <= 0:
        return h, float('inf')

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    cur = float(np.max(corr))
    active = np.where(corr >= cur - 1e-8)[0]
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.time() < deadline - 0.6:
        improved = False

        # Targeted pairs drawn from currently active lags.
        num_target = max(60, n)
        for _ in range(num_target):
            if time.time() > deadline - 0.6:
                break
            if active.size == 0:
                break
            idx = active[int(rng.randint(active.size))]
            d = int(idx - (n - 1))
            if d < 0:
                qs = -d
            else:
                qs = 0
            if d > 0:
                qe = n - 1 - d
            else:
                qe = n - 1
            if qe < qs:
                continue
            q = qs + int(rng.randint(0, qe - qs + 1))
            p = q + d
            if p == q or p < 0 or p >= n or q < 0 or q >= n:
                continue
            h, corr, imp = optimize_pair(h, corr, p, q, active)
            if imp:
                improved = True
                cm = float(np.max(corr))
                active = np.where(corr >= cm - 1e-8)[0]

        # A few deterministic cyclic offsets.
        if time.time() < deadline - 1.5:
            offs = set()
            offs.add(1)
            offs.add(2)
            offs.add(3)
            offs.add(max(1, n // 16))
            offs.add(max(1, n // 4))
            offs.add(max(1, n // 2))
            for off in list(offs):
                off = min(off, n - off)
                if off == 0:
                    continue
                for i in range(n):
                    if time.time() > deadline - 0.8:
                        break
                    j = (i + off) % n
                    if i < j:
                        h, corr, imp = optimize_pair(h, corr, i, j, active)
                        if imp:
                            improved = True
                            cm = float(np.max(corr))
                            active = np.where(corr >= cm - 1e-8)[0]

        # Random pairs for escape.
        num_rand = max(20, n // 2)
        for _ in range(num_rand):
            if time.time() > deadline - 0.6:
                break
            i = int(rng.randint(0, n))
            j = int(rng.randint(0, n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            if i > j:
                i, j = j, i
            h, corr, imp = optimize_pair(h, corr, i, j, active)
            if imp:
                improved = True
                cm = float(np.max(corr))
                active = np.where(corr >= cm - 1e-8)[0]

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 2:
                break
        else:
            no_improve = 0

        if time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
            if abs(float(np.sum(h)) - n / 2.0) > 1e-10:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
            corr = compute_corr(h)
            cm = float(np.max(corr))
            active = np.where(corr >= cm - 1e-8)[0]

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    return h, compute_c5(h)


def optimize_candidate(h, n, deadline, seed):
    if deadline > _RUN_DEADLINE:
        deadline = _RUN_DEADLINE
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c0 = compute_c5(h)

    if deadline - time.time() > 25.0:
        h, c = pair_descent(h, n, deadline - 3.0, seed)
        if c < c0:
            h, c0 = h, c
    else:
        h, c = h, c0

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5(h)
    if c < c0:
        c0 = c
    return h, c0


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _RUN_DEADLINE
    start = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    _RUN_DEADLINE = start + max(0.1, budget_s - 2.0)

    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if len(parent) < 32:
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        pn = 160
        parent = basic_candidate(pn, seed)
        pn = len(parent)

    if parent is not None:
        parent = project_feasible(parent, parent.size)

    best_h = parent.copy()
    best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)
    best_n = len(best_h)

    if time_left() <= 5.0:
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
        final_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)
        try:
            ev = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
            if ev is not None and float(ev) < final_c5:
                final_c5 = float(ev)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return best_h, final_c5, best_n

    cands = []
    add_candidate(cands, best_h, best_n)
    add_candidate(cands, project_anti(best_h), best_n)
    add_candidate(cands, project_sym(best_h), best_n)

    b = best_h
    bb = (b > 0.5).astype(float)
    add_candidate(cands, project_feasible(bb, best_n), best_n)

    add_candidate(cands, random_smooth_candidate(best_n, seed + 101), best_n)
    add_candidate(cands, random_binary_candidate(best_n, seed + 202), best_n)

    # Sort candidate list without using lambdas.
    order = []
    remaining = list(range(len(cands)))
    while remaining:
        mi = remaining[0]
        mv = cands[mi][0]
        for idx in remaining[1:]:
            if cands[idx][0] < mv:
                mv = cands[idx][0]
                mi = idx
        order.append(mi)
        remaining.remove(mi)

    main_deadline = start + max(1.0, budget_s * 0.55)
    if main_deadline > _RUN_DEADLINE:
        main_deadline = _RUN_DEADLINE

    if time.time() < main_deadline:
        weights = [0.46, 0.24, 0.14, 0.09, 0.05, 0.02]
        polish_total = max(30.0, main_deadline - time.time() - 5.0)
        for rank, idx in enumerate(order):
            if rank >= len(weights):
                w = 0.01
            else:
                w = weights[rank]
            slice_s = w * polish_total
            slice_s = min(slice_s, main_deadline - time.time() - 3.0)
            if slice_s < 18.0:
                break
            h, c = optimize_candidate(cands[idx][1], cands[idx][2], time.time() + slice_s, seed + rank * 17)
            if c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_n = len(best_h)
            if time.time() >= main_deadline:
                break

    # Major polish of the current best.
    if time_left() > 100.0:
        slice_s = min(350.0, time_left() - 50.0)
        h, c = optimize_candidate(best_h, best_n, time.time() + slice_s, seed + 9101)
        if c < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_n = len(best_h)

    # Small zero-sum perturbations of the best solution, then re-polish.
    if time_left() > 70.0:
        amps = [0.025, 0.06, 0.12]
        for z, amp in enumerate(amps):
            if time_left() < 35.0:
                break
            rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + 3000 + 7 * z)
            noise = rng.randn(best_n).astype(float)
            noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
            nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(noise))
            if nrm > 0.0:
                noise = noise / nrm
            hp = best_h + amp * noise * float(np.sqrt(best_n))
            hp = project_feasible(hp, best_n)
            slice_s = min(55.0, time_left() - 20.0)
            if slice_s < 12.0:
                continue
            h, c = optimize_candidate(hp, best_n, time.time() + slice_s, seed + 4200 + 11 * z)
            if c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_n = len(best_h)

    # Try several resolutions, low and high.
    if time_left() > 60.0:
        nset = set()
        nset.add(best_n)
        nset.add(max(80, best_n // 2))
        nset.add(max(96, best_n * 3 // 2))
        nset.add(max(128, best_n * 2))
        nset.add(240)
        nset.add(320)
        nset.add(480)
        nset.add(512)
        targets = []
        for nn in nset:
            if nn % 2 == 1:
                nn += 1
            if 96 <= nn <= 512:
                targets.append(int(nn))
        targets = sorted(set(targets))

        tries = 0
        for nn in targets:
            if time_left() < 45.0 or tries >= 4:
                break
            if nn == best_n:
                continue
            h = interp_to(best_h, best_n, nn)
            h = project_feasible(h, nn)
            ha = project_anti(h)
            ca = compute_c5(ha)
            cb = compute_c5(h)
            if ca < cb:
                h = ha
            slice_s = min(65.0, time_left() - 25.0)
            if slice_s < 12.0:
                slice_s = 12.0
            hh, cc = optimize_candidate(h, nn, time.time() + slice_s, seed + 5000 + nn)
            if cc < best_c5:
                best_c5 = cc
                best_h = hh.copy()
                best_n = len(best_h)
            tries += 1

    # Final short polish if time remains.
    if time_left() > 25.0:
        slice_s = min(45.0, time_left() - 3.0)
        h, c = optimize_candidate(best_h, best_n, time.time() + slice_s, seed + 8888)
        if c < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_n = len(best_h)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    try:
        ev = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if ev is not None and float(ev) < final_c5:
            final_c5 = float(ev)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_h, float(final_c5), int(best_n)
```
